- **Auto_dev**: Coordinate a parent-child multi-agent execution loop for this repository. Reads the execution mode (workflow / agent / loop) from .ark/project_goal.md and adapts stopping behavior accordingly. Dispatches workers by task type with a model chosen per task difficulty, verifies results, updates coordination files, and in loop mode checks acceptance criteria with gap-analysis replanning until all criteria pass or an escalation occurs. ([xixi131/Agent-Run-Kit](https://github.com/xixi131/Agent-Run-Kit/tree/HEAD/global-skills/Auto_dev) · ★4)
- **auto_dev_init**: Initialize or migrate the Agent Workflow Kit into a target project. Copies AGENTS.md to the project root and all other workflow files into a .ark/ subdirectory. If the project was previously initialized with the old flat-file layout, migrates existing files from the root into .ark/ automatically. Use when the user invokes $auto_dev_init or asks to set up (or upgrade) a target project with the workflow kit. ([xixi131/Agent-Run-Kit](https://github.com/xixi131/Agent-Run-Kit/tree/HEAD/auto_dev_init) · ★4)
